## 2026-08-29 — Close correlated service transactions and typed failure settlement

`working tree` · area: service call lifecycle and optimistic state

The correlated service transaction path is complete: Luau methods return
CallId-backed tickets, shell and backend transport preserve that identity,
deadlines/cancellation and coalescing report typed terminal outcomes, and
provider results are validated before ticket completion. The final settlement
edge now treats every terminal status other than `completed` as a rollback,
including typed invalid-result, queue-full, stale-generation, and other future
invocation failures. Chained optimistic `stateBinding` writes therefore unwind
to the last provider value without allowing an older completion to overwrite a
newer write. The regression fixture also supplies the strict initial state
required by the audio contract and verifies that the transaction chain is
fully cleared after a malformed provider result.

without displacing the highest valid lower-precedence layer. Settings storage
now retains canonical values, while embedded components distinguish typed
dynamic bindings from type-directed static literals.

**Verification.** The component suite passed (91), surface-config passed (37),
prop-focused shell tests passed (17, 2 ignored), compiler tests passed (18),
`cargo check --workspace`, `cargo fmt --all -- --check`, and `git diff --check`
passed. The full frontend library suite still has one existing unrelated
phase87 `Loading Ready` versus `Main grid` fixture failure (82 passed, 1 failed,
20 ignored). No performance claim is made.

## 2026-08-31 — Derive surface geometry from component-root CSS

`mesh.surface.exclusive_zone` and `mesh.surface.margins` are no longer
manifest, settings, or schema inputs. Top-level surface roots now retain their
resolved CSS margins for compositor placement, derive content size from their
measured border boxes, and derive the anchored exclusive zone from the outer
edge; `exclusive-zone: none` opts overlays out. Shipped surface fixtures now
express geometry in CSS, with regressions covering root-box measurement,
margin placement, manifest rejection, CSS resolution, and the editor contract.

**Verification.** `cargo check --workspace`,
`cargo test --workspace --no-run`, formatting, and `git diff --check` passed.
Focused surface-config (37), surface-policy (8), shell surface-layout (22),
shell measured-geometry (1), compiler navigation-root (1), module manifest
rejection (1), CSS exclusive-zone resolution (1), config roundtrip (1), and
LSP CSS completion (1) tests passed. The full module suite reached 274 passed,
9 existing unrelated/environment failures, and 3 ignored; the full elements
suite reached 250 passed, 10 existing unrelated failures, and 57 ignored. No
performance claim is made.

## 2026-08-31 — Establish unique `NodeId` tree identity and reject duplicate/unknown elements

`WidgetNode` trees now have a shared validation boundary before layout, event
dispatch, frame identity indexing, and accessibility projection. Validation
rejects duplicate `NodeId` values, duplicate stable mesh keys, unknown element
contracts, and explicit source/runtime tag mismatches. `NodeId` allocation now
fails safely at the `u64` limit. Compiler and shell lowering validate produced
trees before they reach runtime consumers, while compatibility APIs fail closed
and expose fallible validation APIs for callers that need diagnostics.

**Verification.** Focused tree, layout, event, accessibility, frame, and
compiler validation tests passed. `cargo check --workspace`, formatting, and
`git diff --check` passed. The full elements suite reached 260 passed, 10
existing style/theme failures, and 57 ignored; the full frontend suite reached
83 passed, 1 existing accessibility-label fixture failure, and 20 ignored; the
full shell suite reached 786 passed, 33 existing integration/config failures,
and 131 ignored. No performance claim is made.

## 2026-08-31 — Close the canonical element-schema backlog item

`working tree` · area: UI element core contracts and pseudo-state matching

The current main history already contains the implementation for this item:
the component `SourceTag` vocabulary and one canonical element schema generate
source/runtime mappings, runtime type definitions, family-specific attributes,
event sets, style hooks, accessibility defaults, and input defaults. Compiler
lowering and core tooling consume that registry. The typed pseudo-state table
supplies canonical names, aliases, dependency bits, state accessors, authored
decoding, indexing, matching, invalidation, and diagnostics. The stale unchecked
backlog entry is removed rather than duplicating the shipped implementation.

**Verification.** Focused element-contract, pseudo-state, style-indexing,
compiler-tag, and gesture-event tests passed. `cargo check --workspace`,

passed, 10 existing style/theme failures, and 57 ignored; the full frontend
suite reached 83 passed, 1 existing source-semantics fixture failure, and 20
ignored. No performance claim is made.

## 2026-08-31 — Consolidate the UI frame transaction

`working tree` · area: UI element core frame coordination

The requested interaction/style/layout/accessibility consolidation is already
implemented in the current main history. `mesh-core-interaction` provides the
staged focus, pointer-capture, press-origin, gesture, and scroll ownership
transaction with typed dirty-node output. Its ordered `InteractionFrame`
coordinates state, style invalidation, retained layout, animation, paint, and
semantic phases. Retained layout preserves last-known-good geometry and marks
failed passes invalid for retry. Post-layout accessibility normalization feeds
the immutable `FrameSnapshot`, whose semantic diff is published with the shell's
completed frontend frame. The stale umbrella backlog entry is removed rather
than duplicating those shipped boundaries.

**Verification.** Focused interaction, frame snapshot, retained-layout
failure/recovery, accessibility, and shell frame-publication tests passed.
The full elements suite reached 260 passed, 10 existing style/theme failures,
and 57 ignored; the full frontend suite reached 83 passed, 1 existing
source-semantics fixture failure, and 20 ignored; the full shell suite reached
786 passed, 33 existing integration/config failures, and 131 ignored. Workspace
checking, formatting, and `git diff --check` passed. No performance claim is
made.

## 2026-08-31 — Unify interaction visibility and target geometry

`working tree` · area: interaction and motion eligibility

Interaction, rendering damage, focus traversal, tooltips, scrolling, and shell
state cleanup now share the post-construction visibility and disabled/inert
eligibility contract. Affine clip intersection uses the painted convex
geometry, degenerate transforms are rejected for geometry-backed targets, and
transformed scrollbar hits use the painted local track with projected drag
coordinates. Key-addressed handlers, stale capture/focus/gesture/scroll state,
and shell damage walks now recheck the same eligibility policy.

**Verification.** Focused interaction-contract (8), accessibility (8), shell
state (1), and transformed-scrollbar/interaction tests passed; the interaction
library reached 42 passed and 12 ignored, and the render library reached 253
passed and 36 ignored. `cargo check --workspace`, formatting, and
`git diff --check` passed. The full elements library reached 263 passed, 10
existing style/theme/props fixture failures, and 57 ignored. The full shell
library reached 785 passed, 34 existing integration/config/fixture failures,
and 131 ignored; the failures include shipped popover, graph/catalog/locale,
service-fixture, animation, gesture, profiler, and retained-layout fixture
coverage outside this item. No performance claim is made.

## 2026-08-31 — Define canonical interaction transaction

`working tree` · area: interaction and motion input/frame policy

The renderer-neutral interaction frame now carries typed invalidation alongside
its state snapshot, decisions, and dirty nodes, preserving those invalidations
when late reconciliation is deferred to the next frame. Pointer press focus
and capture, pointer-outside release and focus clearing, scrollbar capture and
scroll release, and multi-touch cancellation now stage their related ownership
changes in one shell-to-core transaction. Focus event/cache updates remain
post-commit effects, so the core transaction remains the single source of
ownership and invalidation policy.

**Verification.** `mesh-core-interaction` passed 43 tests with 12 ignored;
the focused shell reflow slice passed 11 tests; workspace checking,
formatting, and `git diff --check` passed. The broader shell interaction slice
reached 152 passed, 8 failed, and 18 ignored on the existing animation,
two-finger-scroll fallback, and real-surface fixture baseline failures. No
performance claim is made.

## 2026-08-31 — Complete motion semantics

`working tree` · area: interaction and motion animation

The motion-semantics umbrella is complete in the current implementation.
Keyframe playback preserves progress across pause/resume and iteration
boundaries; animation identity includes node, list slot, and declaration
generation with explicit continuation, replacement, cancellation, and
reversal; reduced-motion policy covers non-essential animation and scrolling;
visibility transitions preserve the displayed discrete value until the proper
endpoint; and per-keyframe easing survives component, theme, and shell
lowering. The remaining transition-longhand comma-list limitation stays as
the separate backlog item below this one.

**Verification.** `mesh-core-animation` passed 37 tests; the focused shell
keyframe slice passed 16 tests and the reduced-motion scroll regression passed
1 test. The broader shell animation slice reached 19 passed, 4 existing
retained-animation baseline failures, and 1 ignored. No performance claim is
made.

## 2026-08-31 — Give transition longhands independent comma lists

`working tree` · area: interaction and motion style resolution

Transition property, duration, delay, and timing-function longhands now retain
independent comma-separated source lists and resolve them into one runtime
entry per property. Shorter timing lists repeat, longer lists truncate, and
the source lists survive later property-list length changes before the
animator receives the resolved entries. Transition shorthands continue to
reset and seed all four lists together.

**Verification.** The focused longhand resolver tests passed (2), the complete
`mesh-core-animation` suite passed (37), the complete style-animation slice
passed (17), and `cargo check --workspace`, formatting, and `git diff --check`
passed. The full elements suite reached 265 passed, 10 existing theme/fixture
failures, and 57 ignored; the focused shell animation slice reached 19 passed,
4 existing retained-scope failures, and 1 ignored; the full shell suite reached
786 passed, 33 existing integration/config/fixture failures, and 131 ignored.
No performance claim is made.

## 2026-08-31 — Close public box-shadow parser and Section 9 regression matrix

`working tree` · area: interaction, animation, and render contract

The public animation `box-shadow` parser is now covered as an explicit
single-shadow contract: supported pixel/color forms interpolate all numeric
and color components, while unsupported lists, malformed functions, invalid
lengths/colors, duplicate keywords, missing fields, and negative blur return
structured errors. Comma-separated shadows remain safely rejected instead of
being truncated. The Section 9 regression matrix is backed by the existing
interaction, render, and shell animation regressions for shared visibility and
transformed geometry, disabled/inert target eligibility, ownership and frame
ordering, motion lifecycle, shadow visual overflow, and sparse damage.
The stale unchecked backlog line is removed because its implementation and
cross-subsystem matrix are present in the current main history.

**Verification.** `nix develop -c cargo test -p mesh-core-animation --lib`
passed (39 tests); `mesh-core-interaction` passed (43 passed, 12 ignored);
`mesh-core-render` passed (253 passed, 36 ignored); the focused elements
box-shadow resolver passed (1, 1 ignored); and `nix develop -c cargo check
--workspace` passed. `nix develop -c cargo test -p mesh-core-shell --lib
animation` reached 32 passed, 4 existing retained-fingerprinting failures,
and 5 ignored (`animation_only_tick_uses_scoped_retained_fingerprinting`,
`animation_scoped_retained_diff_matches_full_pipeline`,
`external_invalidation_cancels_animation_only_retained_scope`, and
`smooth_scroll_animation_uses_scoped_retained_fingerprinting`). No
performance claim is made.

## 2026-08-31 — Complete atomic frontend catalog revisions

`working tree` · area: frontend compiler and host

Primary roots and resolved extension-point roots now compile into one catalog
candidate. Compiler checks require imported interfaces to be declared by the
owning module, while the installed graph validates provider availability and
contract-version compatibility during source reload as well as initial
indexing. Mounted hosts watch contribution sources owned by other modules,
including rejected roots for retry, reload all consumed roots atomically, and
refresh their watch mtimes after catalog changes. The shared reverse
dependency graph covers primary and contribution ownership and host consumers,
including contribution-only dependencies; failed candidates retain the
last-known-good catalog.

**Verification.** Focused source-reload, contribution-root, stale-rollback,
contribution-only-dependency, and source-reload interface-range tests passed
(8, 3, 1, 1, and 1 respectively). The shell catalog slice reached 16 passed,
1 existing graph-locale failure, and 3 ignored; the invalidation slice reached
56 passed, 4 existing retained-fingerprinting/service-fixture failures, and
13 ignored. The full shell library reached 788 passed, 33 existing
integration/config/fixture failures, and 131 ignored. Workspace checking,
format checking, and `git diff --check` passed. No performance claim is made.
